The room follows the window a layout late, so a jump to a narrower window (a programmatic resize, a tile, Zoom) had moved every item into the overflow (») for good; once the width has settled (0.2 s, not during a drag) an empty item is shown for a moment (`AppModel.toolbarWidthChanged`, `toolbarNudge`), a change of the toolbar's items being what makes it bring them back (review fixes, below). The overview's subtitle names the latest round, as there is no round menu there: "Lin Zhou · Round 2 · 6 comments and 1 edit to check"; Review's is §1's ("Lin Zhou · 6 comments and 1 edit to check", its round in the round menu).
- **The overview's toolbar**: ‹ ›, the title and subtitle (from macOS 15 the toolbar's own, as in Review), the engine's progress, **Following Methods ×** while it follows something (Sage words on the accent's tint; the × or Esc or a click on empty space in the overview follows nothing; at the trailing side by More, not after the title as in the mockup's png/04: a deliberate deviation, the native place for a scope token, where it never pushes the title), **More** (⋯: the last closed round's final.pdf, New Round… when no round is open, Show in Finder) and Continue Round N.
- **A paper without a round** has no overview: Review's "No rounds yet" page (§11) shows at either level.

**The data** (`OverviewModel`, `OverviewData.swift`): the engine's read-only `overview` (FEATURES §5.1 with OVERVIEW-v2 §5.2's `prose`, `spans`, `match`, `edits`, `comments[].path`; CONTRACT amendment "App, overview navigation"), `{"papers": [path], "marks": true}`, through the engine process the review uses, written after the paper's `state` request so it is answered after it. It is asked for when the overview is shown for a paper, and again only after a request that may change the paper (`ledgerEdits`: every request but `state`, `prose`, `summaries`, `email_preview` and `overview`) or once a round it had pending is filled in (below, **Pending rounds**); the old data stays shown while the new comes. Every field decodes as optional and the rows of `spans`, `match`, `edits`, `heat` and `path` are read by position, so an engine with more or fewer fields still decodes; ids come as strings whether the engine sends numbers or strings. **Fallback**: an engine without `overview` answers "Unknown method 'overview'." (rpc.py): the overview then shows what the paper's state says (the title, a first sentence from the latest round, the rounds' rails), with one line over the map, "The paper map needs the engine's overview, which this engine does not have yet."; another error says "The overview could not be read: …". Neither raises an alert. `--overview-fixture <file>` reads an `overview` reply instead of asking (its paper by path, else its first); a `--fixture` run without one has none.

**What the engine sends** (2026-10-19, engine side; the exact shapes are CONTRACT amendment "Overview"):
- **Section ids are keys**, strings that stay the same while a section continues across rounds (`front` for Title and abstract; a renamed section keeps its key, a merged one ends, a split's other half or an added section gets a new key from its title). The same key in `spans`, `heat`, `edits`, `path` and `sections[]` is the same section, so following a section is following its key; `match` adds the merges, splits and their evidence. The app also reads ids sent as numbers (`FlexibleID`). `Fixtures/overview-m2a.json` (hand-written before the engine) now has the engine's keys, words and `null` match in Round 1; `Fixtures/overview-kelp.json` is the engine's own reply (with `marks`) for a synthetic four-round paper it built, its paths replaced.
- **Words**: a step's `state` placed / gone / unplaced; `response` new (its creation round), edited, note_only, untouched, waiting; `answer` null (its creation round), resolved, kept, follow_up, left_open, to_check (never the ledger's verified / wontfix / reopened); `match` kinds same, renamed, merged, split, moved, removed (`to` null), added (`from` null); `suggested_bucket` abstract, introduction, method, results, discussion, conclusion or null.
- **Pending rounds** (§5.5): `prose` pending means the round's paragraphs are not found yet (a new folder, or the engine's `PARSER` changed). The app fills closed rounds in from a **second engine process** (FEATURES E1b): `prose` with `round` and `state: false`, one round at a time, then `overview` of that paper again; the main process never waits for it, and it quits with the app. The open round is found at ingest, or by the main process's `prose` as Review does: Review asks for it when the paper's state comes without paragraphs, also while the overview is shown, and the overview, asked before that reply, is asked again once it comes (`AppModel.roundsFound`, `OverviewModel.stale`; integration 2026-10-19: until then an open round left `pending` by the PARSER change stayed "Finding sections…" until the next answer). A round filled in while Review is shown makes the overview ask again when it is shown. `unavailable` (the build failed, or finding the paragraphs failed with this engine: `rN/prose.failed`) is not asked for again.
- **Also there**: `rounds[].unplaced` (the row's "+n unplaced"), `rounds[].excerpts` (an edit's first words, for its tick's help tag), `marks` with `marks: true` (the thumbnails' rects; since the map each names its comment or change, CONTRACT amendment "App, paper map"), `sections[].written`, and `students` for the Student page (`land`, `time`, `closed`; the advisor's Map Headings answers go in as `buckets`). `edit_paper` (paper, title?, student?) renames a paper or its student; `overview` groups papers by the student's name with case and spaces ignored.

**The overview's frame** (`OverviewScreen`, `OverviewViews.swift`), on the panel's colour, inset 24 pt (the mockup's 18 / 24 / 26):
- **Header**: the paper's title in New York 24 Semibold (`PageType.paperTitle`, the hierarchy's page-title size; 20 until §25), one line; the **first sentence** (13, label 2, up to two lines at most 760 pt wide, never cut in one line, whole in its help tag; `OverviewText.firstSentence`, FEATURES OV-1): when the latest round came in (`created`, the import, as every date of the overview and the hierarchy since the review fixes), what the student did with the open passages, without counting ("edited every / most / some / none of the open passages", "and explained one / two / three / several in a note(s)"), and the sections where they changed what no comment asked for ("and changed part of Design that no comment asked about"), the student by first name and no pronoun (§12); "Round 1 came in on …: the first draft."; "Round N went back to Lin on …; nothing to do until the next round comes in."; "… but did not build: 2 errors in methods.tex." (the errors from the state). At the right, 12 secondary: "**17 comments** · 2 rounds since Oct 7" over "2 resolved · 1 kept · 14 open · 4 pp · 3,120 words" (what is known).
- **The panel** under the map while it follows something: below, **Following, the panel, the keys and the table**.
- **Clicks** (`Clicks`): the first click acts at once; a second within the system's double-click interval on the same view opens Review (a TapGesture of count 2 would have delayed every single click).

**The paper map** (2026-10-19; OVERVIEW-v2 §3.3, §4, §5.1, §5.4–5.5, the mockup's `stripHTML`, `paperMapSVG` and `geo`). Under the header, in this order:
- **Pages of Round N** (`PageStripView`, `OverviewStrip.swift`): the strip's round (the latest that built by default, `MapRules.defaultStrip`; a cell, round or page moves it, §8 decision 6), its page count, "○ something to check" while an open round has a comment to check or an edit nobody asked for that waits, and at the right "Click a section, round, page or comment to follow it · double-click opens Review" ("Esc shows the whole paper again" while following). One thumbnail per page in its slot (the overview's width over the pages), at most 72 pt high and 8 pt narrower than its slot, in the pages' own proportion (`StripGeometry`), white in either appearance. Over it the round's marks (`overview` is asked with `marks: true`): a comment as it is drawn on the page, in its colour (a highlight filled at 55 %, 30 % once resolved or kept; a box outlined; an underline, a strikeout; a note's dot), the comment followed outlined in Sage, an amber ring in the margin by one to check; an edit a bar under its first line (green; slate teal when nobody asked for it), with an amber ring while it waits. Under each page its number, with ○ when the page has something to check; in a crowded strip the ○ goes on the page's corner, and when even the numbers do not fit every other one shows (`StripLabels`). Following a section frames its pages in Sage and puts the others at 40 %; a page or a comment frames the pages that hold it. A click follows the page, a double click opens Review there; its help tag counts its comments (to check), its edits and those nobody asked for.
- **The section ruler** (`SectionRuler`) under the strip, at the strip's page scale (under the pages it names; the lens joins it to the map's own scale, below): each first-level section of the strip's round where it starts, after a hairline: its title; a numbered one its number and as much of its name as fits with the map's own name for it, or a longer form of the same stem (`SectionRuler.label(_:room:map:)`, the scene's `names`, built before the strip; the UI audit of 2026-10-21: "1 Intro." over cells saying "Int.", "3 Abst." over "ASP"), else by the map's rule for names (`MapRules.label`, §21 **Cells**: "1 Intro.", "3 Bench."), else its number alone, never a lone letter after it, with or without "." ("3 B" read as an appendix's), and nothing only when not even the number fits; an appendix's section its letter and its name the same way ("A Dat.", "B Eval."); a title without a number its short name ("Abstract") as far as it fits ("Ab."), but never a lone letter, which would read as an appendix's (the advisor's review of 2026-10-20 replaced "its number alone, never a stub"); the section followed on the accent's tint in 12.5 Semibold; "Finding sections…" or "Sections unavailable for this round" without sections. A click follows the section at that round.
- **Thumbnails** (`ThumbnailStore`, `Thumbnails.swift`): drawn off the main thread with CoreGraphics from the round's build PDF (read only), 160 px high for the strip and 400 for a key page, and kept as PNGs in `~/Library/Caches/Paperole/Thumbnails/<hash of the paper's path>/<hash of the PDF's path, size and date>-p<page>-<px>.png` (Paperole Dev uses `~/Library/Caches/Paperole Dev/Thumbnails/`), so a PDF built again is drawn again (the dates are looked at again with each new overview) and **Remove from List** deletes the paper's folder. A test run (`--background`, a fixture, `swift test`) keeps them in a temporary folder of its own, or in `--cache <folder>`. The **key page** of a paper (`MapRules.keyPage`, for the Student and All Students cards, v1b) is the page of a round with most comments and edits (`rounds[].pages`), the first of equals.
- **The map** (`MapScene.build`, pure, in `OverviewMap.swift`; drawn in one Canvas by `MapCanvas`, `OverviewMapView.swift`):
  - **Geometry** (`MapGeometry`): the readout at the right, 320, 214 or 132 pt for three, two or one columns (above); a 46 pt column of "+n unplaced" caps after the rows when any round has one; the map from 108 pt (the rail) to 22 pt before them, one page scale for every row (the paper's most pages, or more once a long appendix folds: §23), which each row then draws its sections through (`MapScale`, **Cells**). The **lens** (30 pt) joins the strip to the map's page axis with hairlines only (v3: no fill, no Sage at rest): 0.75 pt at 6.5 % ink from each section's start in the strip to its place on the map, fewer where they crowd (a start within 24 pt of another on the strip, or 8 pt on the map, has none), the strip's two ends and a folded part's edges always; a folded part a faint wedge (`wedge`); the section or page followed its wedge on the accent's tint, the only Sage in the lens. Then a row every 56 pt (a 28 pt cell, the 8 pt rug of ticks, 20 pt to the next).
  - **Rows**, the latest on top, each the paper in that round. The rail: a grey dot, an amber ring on the open round with something to check, a red one on a failed build (v3: a closed round's dot radius 3 in `railDot`, the rings radius 4.5 at 1.8 pt, the rail's line 1 pt); "Round N" (12.5 Semibold) and its dates; the strip's round on the track's colour (radius 6; the round followed on the accent's tint with a 1.5 pt Sage edge); between rows "↑ n carried", the comments carried into the round above (for a failed round, those waiting for a PDF).
  - **Cells**: each first-level section in page order, from where it starts to where it ends in that round (`spans`), as wide as its length but never narrower than a floor (`MapScale`, the advisor's review of 2026-10-20: "a short name or the first letters is better than none"; asked whether cells should follow the length or all be equal, the answer was to keep the length, which is the map's point and lines it up with the strip, with a floor): the floor holds "Abc." and the paper's largest count (two digits at most) in the narrow cell's spacing (about 48 pt for one digit, 56 for two); the room comes from every section above it in proportion to its length (one factor for the row), so the longest give the most, the row keeps its width and both ends, places stay in page order and a longer section is never drawn narrower than a shorter one; a folded part's block keeps the width the plan gave it (§23), and a quiet segment is one unit of the scale, floored and scaled by its whole length, its sections sharing it by theirs. A row too crowded for the floor (11 sections in about 480 pt at 1280 pt, or a paper with its appendix shown) takes at most 90% of an equal share as its floor, so it is still in proportion above it, but at least the room for its widest count alone when that is under 95% of one. Every place of a row goes through its scale (`RowScale`): cells, bands, ticks, path dots and the hover's preview, the lens's lines, the page axis. Its count (`heat`: written then plus still open from before), 12.5 Semibold; its name at the left (11 Regular, label 2, without the number), 7 pt in with 8 pt to its count, or in a narrow cell 4 pt in with 5 pt to it (`MapRules.CellSpacing`), as much of it as fits (`MapRules.label`, replacing v3's whole-or-nothing): whole; a fixed short form (Intro., Disc., Concl., Related, Exp., Eval., Prelim., Limits, Ack., Ethics for Ethics statement and Ethical considerations, Refs, Methods for Materials and methods; `MapRules.abbreviations`); its first words and "…" ("Benchmark…", never ending on "and", "of" …); its first word cut with "." (`cuts`: of the first word that is not a small word, "Prop." for The Proposed Method, without punctuation round it, before a hyphen, "Pre." for Pre-training; at most five letters, never ending on a vowel or inside "ch", "th", "ck" …, never leaving off fewer than three letters, four of a word of eight or more, as "Relat.", "Datas.", "Abstr." read as misspellings, never one letter short of its fixed form, "Intr." beside "Intro.": "Bench.", "Eval.", "Prel.", down to two letters, "Be."; Chinese or Japanese cut with "…"); the initials of a name of three or more words that are not small words ("ASP"; two read as a word, "ES", and are only a way out of a look-alike, below); its first letter with "." ("B.": a lone capital reads as an appendix's letter, or as "I", the digit 1); its first letter alone, last (the advisor: nothing only when not even one character fits), but never "I" or "l" alone beside a count, which reads as 11 or 13: the count alone then (UI audit 2026-10-21). A lettered appendix section (its title's letter, in the appendix part) leads with its letter, as the paper refers to it, and ends on it: "C Experimental Details", "C Exp.", "C". One name per section for every row, cut to the narrowest room it has in any (beside its count, `SectionName.base`); a wider cell shows a longer form of the same stem, or its whole name or fixed short form ("Ben." in the narrowest, "Bench." or "Benchmark…" where there is room: `MapRules.extends`, `pick`; the UI audit: wide cells showed the narrowest's stub), so it still reads as the same section down the rounds; a cell where not even a letter fits that room has its own. No cut name that reads as another section's (one starts with the other, `MapRules.clash`) while another of its forms fits, the rows from the top and each from the left: "Abs." for the abstract, then "ASP", not "Abst.", for Ablation as Sensitivity Probe; when every form that fits does, its initials if they read as none ("AI" for Abstract Interpretation, `MapRules.initials`); a whole name or its fixed short form is always its own; a longer form in a wider cell never reads as another section's name either (`SectionName.avoid`: "A." stays "A." beside an abstract named "Ab.", lone letters aside). **A quiet segment is named too** (the UI audit: its blank stretch read as the names missing): its sections' names in an equal share each, joined by " · " ("Rel. · Disc."), none a lone letter, else the first's and how many more ("Related +1"), else the first's as far as it fits (`MapScene.quietName`), 4 pt in, in the name ink. The count is never hidden by the name; its shade one of three warm-stone steps chosen per paper (`MapShade`: thirds of the paper's own non-zero counts, unfolded; 1, 2, 3+ while no count passes 3), the near-neutral track for none. No cell has an outline: one corner radius (4) and 2 pt of the panel between neighbours (each 1 pt in). Neither changes with what is followed.
  - **A count is never covered** (`MapRules.placeNumber`, the mockup's rule): 7 pt from the right with a name at the left, in the middle without one (v3), else left, right, or beside a path dot, never within 9.5 pt of one; with no free place the dot moves to the cell's lower edge. It is drawn after everything inside the rows (with a halo of its cell's shade beside a path). **Every section is drawn at least as wide as its count** (the floor above; until 2026-10-20 the room came from a counted section's nearest neighbours only: real papers have sections a twentieth of a page long holding a comment). Only in a row so crowded that not even the count's room is under 95% of an equal share does a count wider than its cell run over the cell's edge, never over a neighbour's count or name, else it is left out (`keepNumbersApart`) and the help tag and VoiceOver still say it.
  - **To check** (the open round only): an amber ○ chip with the cell's comments still to check, a 16 pt capsule 3 pt above the cell (v3: no longer on its corner), its right edge at the cell's (centred over a cell under 44 pt), its number amber; chips closer than 4 pt are pushed right, inside the map (`MapScene.spread`). They add up to the cells' comments to check; one the round could not place is in the toolbar's count, not in a chip.
  - **Edits** (`edits`): one tick each at its place, in the rug under the cell, at 85 % (v3): a 1.5 × 5 pt green bar when a comment asked for it, a 1.5 × 2.6 pt slate-teal bar with a 3 pt dot when nobody did (a section's move too, which prints no words), an amber ring while it waits for you. An edit without a place has none.
  - **Bands** join each section to its place in the round below (`match`; only between consecutive rounds that both have sections): one quiet tone with no edge, 1 pt in from each end (v3; the critique's values, so a band stays at least ΔE00 2.3 from the panel and from the track: ink 5.5 %, white 3.5 % in Dark Mode); whether it moved, merged, split, was renamed or changed length is said by its shape (a slanted band, two into one, one into two, wider at one end) and by "moved", "merged into Methods", "renamed" or "split" (once for both halves) in the gap (11 Regular secondary with a halo of the panel, no box) — the darker "changed" tone of before could not be kept apart from both the bands and the track (deviation, the advisor to confirm); a removed section's band ends in a point ("removed"); an added section has none below it ("added"). A label sits centred under its band, else beside it with a short leader, else a little lower or further along (`LabelPlacer`): never over another label, a pill, an "unplaced" caption or another removed band's point; one that steps back while another section is followed is left out when it finds no room. When one of a gap's labels finds no room, the gap says them all in one label ("9 renamed", "2 moved · 1 renamed"; `placeGap`), else nothing (the bands' help tags still say each); a label is never drawn where it clashes, and never in red.
  - **Row ends**: a shorter round ends with a cap and "12 pp" (when the words fit); the comments a round could not place (orphans, no page) are "+n" in a cap after the row with "unplaced" under it (11 secondary), the cap shaded by its count as a cell (the critique: the track would say no comments), counted in the row's Comments and in no cell. With folded parts the cap and its page count show only for a round of fewer pages that ends visibly earlier.
  - **Rows without sections** (§5.5): `prose` pending: the round's pages only (a track with a 1.2 pt gap of the panel between its pages, its caption on the track with a halo, no box), a dot for each comment placed, the edits' ticks, and "Finding sections…"; unavailable, or `ok` without spans: "Sections unavailable for this round". No band goes to or from such a row. **A failed build**: a dashed red outline over the row, its ⚠ red and its words in the label colour, "⚠ Build failed · 2 errors in methods.tex. 9 comments wait for a PDF." (the errors from the state when it is that round's), and in the readout "9 comments wait for a PDF" over "Show Log… in the toolbar"; the strip shows the latest round that built; the first sentence ends "but did not build: 2 errors in methods.tex."
  - **Folding** (§8 decision 12): when the map would run past the window's bottom (its top measured in the scrolled overview, 52 pt kept for the legend), the oldest rounds fold into one row: "Rounds 1–2" and "2 older rounds folded: 42 comments written, 26 edits · **Show all 8 rounds**" (`MapRules.folded`: at least two fold and at least two stay, one of a three-round paper; fewer than three never fold). A click unfolds (the overview's `unfold`, kept with its place in the history); **Fold** under the axis folds again.
  - **The page axis**: "page" and each page's number under its middle in the strip's round (else the top row with sections), through that row's scale, so a page of short sections is wider than one of a long section (every other one, 1, 3, 5 …, when the narrowest page drawn is under 20 pt; never one within 3 pt of the last); 11 pt (`MapFont.caption`, 10.5 until 2026-10-21: §19, nothing under 11). The lens's hairlines end at the same row's scale (the code audit: a strip round folded or without sections fell back to the plain page scale, at which no row is drawn). A row's "30 pp" is in the secondary colour (it was tertiary, kept for placeholders).
  - **The readout** (§4.6), always the round's own numbers, in three columns from 1360 pt of overview (a window of about 1650 pt), two (Comments, You) from 816, else one (`OverviewLayout.readoutColumns`; it had three from 1116, and at 1440 and 1280 the readout's 320 pt were the room the map's cells needed for their names, UI audit 2026-10-21: the student's column is in the round's help tag): Comments (the count, 12.5 Semibold, over "n new" or "all new"); the student's, three columns only ("4 edited · 1 note only" over "1 untouched · 1 waiting"; "first draft"); You ("✓ 8 · ↩ 2 · = 1" over "3 left open", or "3 left open" alone when none was answered; "✓ 2 · ↩ 1 so far" in the open round, "none yet" before the first answer, "ready to finish" when nothing is left to check; "–" only for a round with nothing to answer; nothing in Round 1); a round without comments says "0" alone, not "all new"; each line cut with "…" to its column, a failed round's too ("9 wait for a PDF" over "Show Log…" in one column). Captions "Comments", the student's first name, "You".
  - **Following** (§5.1): a section: its cells outlined in Sage in every round, its bands Sage with "n carried" pills (a band that carried none hollow), its wedge in the lens, the rest at 30 % (42 % in Dark Mode); a round: the other rows at 38 % and its rail outlined; a page: its place in its round tinted, the paths of its comments; a comment: its path (○ written, ● carried, the amber ring still to check, ✓ or = closed, ⊗ its passage deleted, □ not placed in that round, a dot on the fold for the rounds folded), its cells in full and the edits made for it stronger, the others at 25 %. Under each round's readout a **Sage line** says what the thing followed is there ("6 here · 5 edited · ✓ 3 · ↩ 1 · 1 open · +120 −90", "p.7 · edited (1 edit) · ✓ resolved · +8 −6", "p.8: passage deleted · ✓ resolved", "p.9: 1 comment · 0 edits"), its tail dropped to fit, under a Sage caption ("Methods only", "R1-13 only").
  - **Clicks and help tags** (`PaperMapView`): the scene says what is under the pointer (`MapScene.target`: path dots, ticks and chips over cells, cells over bands; the rail; the readout). A click follows it: a cell or band its section at that round (a removed band its section the round before), a rail, a readout, an unplaced cap or a row without sections its round, a path dot its comment, a tick its section; a click on empty map follows nothing; a second click on the same thing within the double-click interval opens Review there. While the app is active the cell under the pointer gets a dashed ring and its row its section names above it (`MapScene.hoverNames`: each from its cell's start as far as the next name, cut with "…" or left out, so no two touch; one that would cover a ○ chip, a structure label, its leader or a pill is left out), and after 0.35 s (at once while another tag was just shown) a help tag of the overview's own (`MapHelp`, `OverviewHover`; an NSView help tag waits a second and cannot point at part of a Canvas): a cell's pages and length, what it was the round before (renamed, merged), its comments this round and over all rounds each once, the student's responses, its words and edits, what is to check; a band's evidence ("Merged: 9 of its 10 paragraphs now sit in 2 Methods") and the comments it carried; a round's numbers; why a row has no sections; the unplaced comments' ids; an edit's kind, section, the comments it was made for, its words and first words (`excerpts`); a path dot's comment and what happened to it there; the fold.
  - **VoiceOver**: below, **Following, the panel, the keys and the table**.
  - **Colours** (`MapInk`, VISUAL v3 §3 with the critique's values, the light ramp calmed in §25): the track #E2E1DC and the steps #D5D3CE #C7C5BE #B8B5AE (LCh chroma 2.6–3.9 at hue about 100, the panel's grey-green family; the sand ramp #E4E2DC #DAD4C8 #CDC5B6 #BEB5A5 until §25) (Dark Mode #2D2E2C, #383632 #44413C #54504A, chroma ≤ 4.1, the number always the label colour), bands ink 5.5 % (white 3.5 %), the band followed Sage 22 % (#7CA080 26 %), panel → band → track → steps each ΔE00 ≥ 2.3 in one order of lightness (`MapFoldTests.testTheMapsColours`), Sage #566B58 only for what is followed, amber only for ○, red only for a failed build, green only for edits asked for, slate teal for those nobody asked for.
- **The legend** (`MapLegend`), one line that wraps: this paper's three steps, each a 12 pt swatch and its range ("1–3 · 4–5 · 6+"), "comments on a section, this round", "○ 2 to check", the ticks "edits: asked for · nobody asked", a band "same section a round apart", while a part is folded a block with its chevron "folded: click to show", and **Full key** (a popover with the map's rules in words, folding and quiet segments among them).
- **Checked** (2026-10-19): `MapLayoutTests` (synthetic: `Fixtures/overview-kelp.json`, the engine's reply for a four-round paper it built with a merge, a rename, a split, a move, a removed appendix and an added Conclusion; the M2a fixture; a generated stress paper of 17 sections over 8 rounds and 22 pages; the kelp paper with rounds pending): the geometry by width, the steps, the fold, the key page, where a count goes, fitting words, the strip, the scene's cells, chips, labels, readout and ticks, what a click finds, a failed build, rows without sections, folding and unplaced comments, following, the help tags, the thumbnails' folder and its clearing, and a click and a double click through the hosting view; and the mockup's visibility rule over every state (three widths × nothing, each section, round, comment and page followed × folded or not; about 950): every non-zero count drawn and inside its cell unless the cell is narrower than the count, never under a chip, label, pill, path point, name or another count, in full when its cell holds what is followed; no structure label without a place or over another; the readout the same whatever is followed; nothing past the overview's width. The copies, caches and screenshots were deleted after.

**Following, the panel, the keys and the table** (2026-10-19, the third step; OVERVIEW-v2 §5.1, §7 "跟随", "面板", "表格视图、VoiceOver、地图的键盘导航"; the mockup's `panelHTML`, `tipHTML`, `previewPath`, `png/04`–`08b`; `OverviewPanels.swift`, `OverviewHover.swift`, `OverviewAccess.swift`, `OverviewTable.swift`):
- **What can be followed, and from where** (§5.1's table): a section (a cell, a band, a name on the ruler, a section's name or cell in the table, a panel's Follow button, an edit's tick or line: its section in its round), a round (its rail, its readout, an unplaced cap, a row without sections, a round's heading in the table), a page (its thumbnail), a comment (a path dot, a mark on a thumbnail: the smallest comment mark within 2.5 pt of the click, `StripGeometry.target`; a panel's line or id chip). The strip goes to the round of what is followed (a comment: the strip's round when it is there, else the last round it has a page in, `Comment.shownRound`), and a round folded on the map unfolds. What each changes on the map, the strip and the readout is **The paper map**'s **Following**; the "n carried" pills of two bands into one section (a merge) sit side by side, never one over the other (`MapScene.spreadPills`), and a label set beside its band has a leader that starts past the band's pill and crosses no other label (`LabelPlacer`).
- **The panel** under the legend (`OverviewPanel.make`, pure; `OverviewPanelView`), after a hairline: a header with the thing's name (14 Semibold), one line of facts (12 secondary, cut with "…", whole in its help tag) and its buttons (plain: the toolbar's Continue Round N stays the one Sage button; a long title cut at 300 pt, whole in its help tag); never the replies back and forth, which are Review's (§5.1 "不和审阅重复"):
  - **a section**: "2 Materials and methods · 9 comments over all rounds, each counted once · 5 still open · called Methods until Round 3"; **Open Round 4 at Materials and methods** (the strip's round when the section is there, else its last); then every comment ever in it, each once, as lines in two columns (one under 820 pt): its mark colour, id, the advisor's words ("A box with no note" without any), and "R1–R4 · ○ to check", "R1–R2 · ✓ resolved", "R1–R3 · kept as is", "R1–R3 · left open", "open", "passage deleted" (the closed states in words since 2026-10-21: "– R2" right after "R1–R2" read as a range, and the rounds' last is the round it ended in); after them the edits nobody asked for in it while the latest round is open ("Edit · its first words · p.4 · ○ to check", the slate-teal bar).
  - **a round**: "Round 2 · Sep 19 → 22 · closed · Mei: 13 edited · 1 note only · 4 untouched · +412 −236 words · 17 edits, 2 nobody asked for" (Round 1: its words and pages; a failed build: how many comments wait for a PDF); **Open Round 2 in Review (read-only)** (plain "in Review" for the open round); its comments as id chips grouped by how they ended there: New in this round, then ✓ Resolved, ↩ Followed up, = Kept as is, Left open (an open round: ○ To check, Resolved so far, Followed up so far, Kept as is so far, and the Edits nobody asked for as chips with their page and first words). Every comment of the round is in one group.
  - **a page**: "Page 9 · Round 4 · 4 Discussion · 1 comment · 0 edits"; **Open page 9 in Review**; its comments as lines and the edits nobody asked for on it, or "No comments on this page in this round."
  - **a comment**: "R1-13 · 3 Results · p.7" (or "not placed in Round 4", "p.8: passage deleted"); **Follow Results** and **Open R1-13 in Round 4**; the advisor's words in New York 15 (selectable); "Written in Round 1 (Sep 8) · on the map for 4 rounds · ✓ resolved in Round 4" (or ○ to check in, = kept as is in, open in, open after, passage deleted in); then the table **What ⟨student⟩ changed for it**, a row per round after the first: where (p.7, "–" when not placed), the edits made for it ("1 edit · +17 −10 words", "1 edit · no printed words") or the student's one word (edited, note only, untouched, waiting for you, passage deleted, not placed in this round), the first words of its first edit (`excerpts`), and your answer (✓ resolved, ↩ followed up, = kept as is, left open, ○ to check); under it "The replies themselves are in Review, on the comment's card."
  - A click on a line or chip follows the comment, a double click opens Review at it; a click on an edit's line or chip follows its section; the pointer on a line or chip shows its help tag and previews the comment's path on the map. A click on the panel's own space keeps what is followed (a click on the rest of the overview's empty space follows nothing).
- **Help tags everywhere** (`OverviewHover`, `OverviewTipLayer`): the map's things, the strip's pages and their marks, the ruler's names, the panel's lines and chips, the table's cells and headings, each with the map's words (`MapHelp`; a page: its comments, to check, edits and those nobody asked for). The tag is drawn over the scroll view in the overview's visible frame (`OverviewLayout.viewport`): under and right of the pointer, above it near the bottom, never past the right edge, never cut by the scroll view. Only while the app is active, as the system's.
- **A comment's path previewed** (§5.1 "悬停一条意见，地图上用虚线预览它的路径", the mockup's `.path.prev`): the pointer on a comment (a panel's line or chip, a mark on a thumbnail, a path dot of one not drawn) draws its path on the map dashed (1.8 pt, 4 on 3 off, at 90 %) through the same places a followed path takes (`MapScene.previewPoints`: the row scales, the unplaced cap, a point on the fold), unless it is drawn already. It moves no count: it goes with the pointer.
- **Clearing**: Esc, the toolbar token's ×, a click on empty map or on the overview's empty space.
- **The keys** (`MapKeys.move`, pure; `AppModel.overviewMove`): a click in the overview gives the map the keys (`OverviewKeyView`, an empty first responder behind the whole overview that takes no click; the sidebar's list gives them up as it does to any window content; it is in the window's key view loop, but whether Tab from the sidebar reaches it, which depends on Keyboard navigation and SwiftUI's own focus, was not checked); Back or All Rounds from Review gives them back when nobody has them (never from the sidebar). With the keys: ← → move along a round (a section's neighbours, a page's, the comments of the strip's round in their order on its pages; a round: its first or last section), ↑ ↓ to the round above or below (a section by its key, else where its paragraphs went, the band's other end with most paragraphs, else the section at the same place; a round without sections is followed as a round; a page the same page; a comment through its rounds, the strip going with it), Return opens Review there as a double click, Esc follows nothing. With nothing followed a first arrow follows the strip round's first section. A move into a folded round unfolds the map; the overview then scrolls as little as needed to show the row followed, its chips and its Sage line (`OverviewModel.revealRow`, `scrollToShow`; also when following something unfolds; review fixes); VoiceOver says where it is (an announcement, `MapSpeech.item`). No arrow wraps; one that has nowhere to go is not taken.
- **The table view** (`MapTable.make`, pure; `MapTableView`): the legend's table button (a toggle, `tablecells`; help "Show the paper map as a table") shows the map as a native grid with the same numbers, the viewer's choice kept in the app's defaults (`overviewMapAsTable`; a test run reads and saves none): a row per first-level section in the paper's order (the latest round's; a section that left the paper over the section after it there, a merged one over the one it went into), a column per round, oldest first (its dates; "build failed" in red); each cell the map's count on its shade with "○ 2" in the open round, or "merged" / "removed" in the round it left, "…" while the round's sections are not known, empty before it was written; "each once" at the end. Under them the readout's numbers per round: Unplaced, Comments ("20 · 5 new"), To check, the student's responses, You, Edits ("17 · 2 nobody asked for"), Words. What is followed stands out as on the map: the section's row and the round's column on the accent's tint, the cells holding the comments followed outlined in Sage. Clicks, double clicks, help tags and the keys are the map's; the legend then shows only the steps, ○ and Full key.
- **VoiceOver** (§7 "VoiceOver（每行一句话）"; `MapSpeech`): each row of the map is a group named "Round 4"; in it the rail, one sentence ("Round 4, Oct 16, open. 12 comments, none new. Tomás: 3 edited, 3 untouched, 6 waiting. You: 2 resolved so far, 10 to check. Introduction 1, Materials and methods 4, …"; a failed build, a row without sections say so), a button that follows the round with "Open Round 4 in Review" as a second action; then each cell in one sentence ("Materials and methods, Round 4: 6 comments, none new, 5 to check. Pages 1 to 2. 3 edits."), a button that follows it, "Open in Review" its second action, selected while followed. The strip's pages ("Page 3 of Round 4. 2 comments · 1 edit"), the ruler's names, the panel's lines and chips and the table's cells are elements of their own with the same actions; the table is the reading for whoever prefers a grid.

**Debug hooks** (§13): `--level overview|review`, `--overview-fixture <file>`, `--cache <folder>` (this run's thumbnails), `--no-list` (the Papers list shows only the `--paper` papers: papers.json is neither read nor saved; screenshots without the user's list); items `nav` (with `map`: the table shown, whether the map has the keys, the rounds folded, the hover's target, the comment previewed, the help tag's lines and place, the panel's title, facts and buttons), `paperopen <path> [review]`, `back` / `forward`, `allrounds`, `continue`, `ovclick` / `ovopen <item>`, `ovscroll <y>`, `ovwait`, `ovhover cell:<id>@<n> | round:<n> | edit:<id>@<n> | dot:<comment>@<n> | at:<x>,<y> | comment:<id> | off` (the pointer there through the map's own hover handler, its tag at once, whether the app is active or not: a background run's window gets no pointer), `ovkey left|right|up|down|return` (as typed: routed only while the map has the keys), `ovtable on|off` (the legend's table button); `key left` / `key right` now exist too. Since the integration (2026-10-19) `ovclick`, `ovopen` and `ovkey` also give `timing`: the map scene's build and its Canvas draw in ms (`MapTiming`) and `shownMs`, from the event to the end of the map's next draw (null when it was not drawn again); and **`ovtext`** gives what the overview shows, in words: the first sentence and the facts, the map as drawn (each row's rail, every cell with its count and the number drawn, or null where it was left out, the chips, the ticks by kind, "n carried", "+n unplaced", the row's caption, the readout lines and the Sage line), the structure labels, the legend's steps, each row's VoiceOver sentence, the table's rows and totals, and `loadMs`, from the `overview` request to its data in the model. **Tests** (`NavigationTests.swift`, synthetic: `Fixtures/overview-m2a.json`, the `state-m2a.json` paper's overview, whose rows' cells and unplaced comments add up to each round's count): the history, the reply's shape and its fallback, the first sentence, the facts and the rows, a reply as the engine wrote it (`Fixtures/overview-engine-sample.json`, a synthetic paper, its paths replaced), the levels in a fixture run (Review by default, the overview's subtitle and button, clicks and keys, the positions, Back / Forward / All Rounds with the overview's place and Review's selection, a position waiting for its state, a paper without rounds), the scroll kept for Back, a click and a double click, Review hidden in AppKit without the sidebar.

**Checked** (the third step, 2026-10-19): `MapInteractionTests` (synthetic: `Fixtures/overview-kelp.json`, the stress paper, the M2a fixture): the keys' moves (along a round, across rounds by key, by a merge and a split, to the nearest place, rounds, pages, a comment through its rounds, nothing followed), each panel (a section's comments each once with their ends, its renamed name, its waiting edits and button; a round's groups holding each of its comments once, To check and the edits nobody asked for in the open round, Round 1's words and pages; a page's comments; a comment's facts, its round-by-round table with the edits' words and first words, a deleted passage, Review's round by the strip), the table (its cells and unplaced adding up to each round's comments over the three papers, the latest round's order, merged and removed, what is followed, its totals), the VoiceOver sentences, the previewed path (the followed path's places, the fold), the carried pills never over each other and the leaders over no label in every state at three widths, what a point on the strip is over; and through the model: the arrows taken only while the map has the keys, Return into Review and Back following the same thing with the strip's round, a comment through its rounds, a key into a folded round, a panel line's double click and Back, the table's switch saving nothing in a test run, the help tag. Fixed on them: the help tag's place (the layer had no frame of its own), the hovered row's names over each other, two "n carried" pills over each other on a merge, a leader through another label in a narrow window, the toolbar's token pushed into the overflow at 960 pt by a long section title (the title gives it room by the name's width), the panel's long Follow button, the keys lost after Back and with the table. Not seen on screen: hover with a real pointer and VoiceOver itself (a background run is never active, and this session could not use the accessibility API); a short manual look is needed. The copies, caches and screenshots were deleted after.

**Review fixes** (2026-10-19, after the integration's data and interaction review and its visual review; tests `OverviewReviewTests`, `HierarchyReviewTests`; `swift test` 445 and the engine's 284 pass; checked on background runs of our own bundle with synthetic papers and a demo copy under made-up names):
- **The toolbar after a resize**: a jump to a narrower window had moved every toolbar item into the overflow until another paper was chosen; it is laid out again once the width settles (above, **Title and subtitle**). Checked on background runs: 1440 → 960 → 1280 with a 114-character title, in both levels, every item visible (`nav`'s `toolbar`: the items and those visible).
- **Structure labels**: a label that found no place was drawn anyway, centred and red, over the others (nine renames in one round at 960 pt); now a gap whose labels do not all fit says them in one ("9 renamed"), and a removed band's point is kept clear of the other labels (above, **Bands**).
- **The hovered row's names** kept off the open round's ○ chips, the labels, their leaders and the pills (above, **Clicks and help tags**).
- **The followed row in view** after a key or an unfold (above, **The keys**).
- **One date for "came in"**: the import (`created`) in the first sentence, the rail, the table's headings, the panels, VoiceOver and the comment's "Written in Round 1 (date)", as the facts, Your Move, the timeline and the Student page already said; when the student's files were last changed another day, the round's help tag says "Files last changed Oct 10" (FEATURES E3 had put `received` on the round cards' dates; with one date for every level it moved to the tag).
- **A failed last round**: the facts give the pages and words of the round the strip shows (the latest that built), not "0 pp"; its readout is cut to its column ("9 wait for a PDF" over "Show Log…" in one column).
- **Names**: a lone appendix's letter is dropped by the engine's own name for the section (`PaperOverview.shortTitle`: "Extra", not "A Extra"); the Sage caption names a followed section by its title in the round followed, as the token does; the ruler keeps numbers (above); the readout's words (above); the fold counts "1 comment written"; a round without comments is "0", not "0, all new" (VoiceOver too); the table's headings are "R9" in a column too narrow for "Round 9"; a band followed by its heading only says "Followed by its heading: none of its 2 paragraphs are left"; a cell's help tag shows the student's added words ("+120") in the edits' green.
- **Redraw**: the map is a new Canvas for every scene (`.id(scene.stamp)`): in one background run of six the old Canvas had kept its frame though it drew the new scene; not seen again in the runs after (it could not be made to happen on purpose).
- **Dark Mode thumbnails** at about 96 % white (the strip's and the hierarchy's key pages), as the mockup's.
- **The debug items** `ovtext` and `nav` give the first sentence with the failed build's errors, as drawn (`AppModel.overviewBuildErrors`); `nav` gives the toolbar's items and those visible, and how many times it was laid out again.
- **Deliberate deviations kept**: the Following token at the toolbar's trailing side (above); the overview's Continue Round N looks grey and disabled in every background screenshot (an inactive window), so its Sage fill was not seen.

**Not yet** (OVERVIEW-v2 §7, the next items): Topics in the panel (v2, with tags), Set as Key Page, and opening the app again at the last place. The second engine process (FEATURES E1b) came with the hierarchy (§22 "Pending rounds"). (2026-10-20: Topics, following a tag, Set as Key Page and the last place are built: §24.)


## 22. The hierarchy: students, Your Move and a student's page (OVERVIEW-v2 v1b)
2026-10-19, `design/overview/v2/OVERVIEW-v2.md` §3.1–3.2 and §8 (decisions 8 and 10, "as recommended"), FEATURES OV-7–OV-9 and E2; mockup `png/01`, `02`, `11`, `12`. Synthetic data only in the repo (`Fixtures/overview-students.json`, below).

**Pages** (`HubPage`, `AppModel.hub`, set only by `setHub`): **All Students**, **Your Move** and a **student's page**, each shown instead of a paper: no paper is selected meanwhile (`openHub` leaves the paper shown as a click on another paper does: its editor saved, a new comment's note keeps it), so the detail shows `HubScreen` (`HubViews.swift`), on the panel's colour, inset 24 pt. A launch without `--paper` opens at All Students when the list has papers (the home page; Remove from List of the paper shown goes there too), else the empty page of §11. The window's history (§21) holds the pages as places (`NavPlace.hub`, with the page's scroll), so ‹ › and ⌘[ ⌘] go between pages, overviews and Review; the Go menu has All Students and Your Move too. The toolbar: ‹ ›, the page's title and one line under it ("All Students" · "4 students · 8 papers · 21 rounds"; "Your Move" · "5 papers wait on you, oldest first · 1 build failed"; "Mei Lin" · "2 papers · 6 rounds"), the engine's progress, and New Paper… on All Students.

**One count** (decision 10): a paper **waits on you** while its latest round is open, a failed build included (`PaperStatus.yourMove`: to check, the first draft, paragraphs to read, ready to finish, build failed); with the student once the round is closed; **finished** once marked so. The sidebar's Your Move badge, All Students' heading ("5 papers wait on you", "4 to review or finish · 1 build failed" under it) and Your Move's subtitle are one list, `AppModel.yourMove` (`Hierarchy.yourMove`), from the papers' summaries, so they always say the same number; the earliest import first.

**The sidebar** (`Sidebar`, `SidebarViews.swift`; FEATURES OV-7; the mockup's `sidebarHTML`): Jump to…; **All Students**; **Your Move** with the amber ○ and the count in the system's grey badge (none at 0); then **Students**: each student a row (the fold's chevron, a person, the name, 13 Medium) that opens the student's page, with their papers under it, one line each (13): the title and at its right the count of comments and edits to check in the grey badge (not on the open paper, as before), or a sign, ✓○ ready to finish, ⌛ with the student, a seal for finished (the title grey too, and listed after the student's other papers), ⚠ red build failed, • the first draft, ▍ olive paragraphs to read; the help tag says the status in words ("Round 4 · 3 comments and 5 edits to check"). The second line of the old Papers list ("○ To check · Mei Lin") and its folded header ("○ 2 more to check") are gone: the student is the group and Your Move counts what waits. Students are grouped as the engine groups them (`StudentName.key`: compatibility forms, case and all white space ignored), named as the first listed paper spells it, in alphabetical order; papers without a student under **Unassigned** at the end (no page). A chevron folds a student's papers; the folded students are remembered (`foldedStudents`, never by a test run). A click on a paper selects it and shows its overview, a double click or Return Review, as in §21; in **Review the tree folds to the open paper** (`SidebarLayout.rows`, `reviewPaper`): All Students, Your Move, the student's name as a header with a menu (⌃⌄) of the student's page, their other papers and All Students, the paper, then Contents (§18), whose rows the follower finds through `sidebarRows`. Context menus: a paper's Open in Review, **Edit Paper…** (Assign to a Student… without one), **Mark as Finished** while it is with the student (Mark as Not Finished after), Show in Finder, Remove from List; a student's Open ⟨name⟩'s Page, **Rename Student…**, Hide / Show Papers.

**Names** (FEATURES E2; CONTRACT amendments "Overview" and "App, hierarchy"): `summaries` now names each paper's title and student, so the list groups every paper by its ledger's student at launch and on activation without loading it (`adoptNames`; the list is saved when it changes, never for a --paper paper or by a test run); the hierarchy's `overview` does the same. **Edit Paper…** (`EditPaperSheet`): the title and the student, typed or picked from the students listed; Save sends `edit_paper` with what changed (a student's name cannot be emptied, as the engine refuses it). **Rename Student…** (`RenameStudentSheet`): every paper of the student gets the name (`renameStudent`, one `edit_paper` each); another listed student's name puts both students' papers together; the page and the fold follow the new name. **Mark as Finished** keeps today's date app side (`PaperRef.finished` in papers.json, FEATURES OV-7: not in the ledger); it shows while the latest round is closed (a new round makes the paper your move again).

**The data** (`HubModel`, `AppModel.loadHub`): the engine's `overview` of every listed paper, without marks, with the advisor's Map Headings answers as `buckets` (`AppModel.hubRequest`; "" sent as null), through the review's engine process; asked for when a page is shown and the list, the answers, a paper (any request but the read-only ones) or the app's activation changed since. The reply's summaries and names are taken at once. The pages show what they have meanwhile ("Reading the papers…" in the toolbar). A --overview-fixture is read instead (tests).

**Pending rounds** (FEATURES E1b; §21 "What the engine sends"): a **second engine process** (`Backfill`) fills in the closed rounds an overview reports `pending` (each paper's, oldest first, only rounds with a build PDF), one at a time with `prose` (`round`, `state: false`), and the overview shown is asked again: the paper's after each round, the hierarchy's after each paper. The review's engine never waits for it; it starts with the first round to fill and ends with the app. A round is asked once per run (a failure the engine notes as `rN/prose.failed`: unavailable). The open round is still found at ingest or by Review, so after a PARSER change an open round shows plain pages on the small maps until its paper has been opened (overview or Review); every round filled in, by either process, makes the hierarchy's next request a new one (`roundsFound` is in its key), so a page shown later never keeps a round pending that was filled in meanwhile.

**All Students** (`AllStudentsPage`; OVERVIEW-v2 §3.1, C quantified; the mockup's `allStudentsView`):
- At the left: "This week · Oct 13–19" (the last seven days), the ○ and "5 papers wait on you" (24 Bold, `PageType.title`, as the student's and the paper's page; 30 until §25), "4 to review or finish · 1 build failed", then one sentence (`HubText.thisWeek`, 15, titles Semibold): the rounds that came in this week by paper ("Priya’s **Mangrove sediment cores** (Tue)", "(3 rounds)" for more than one, at most five papers then "and n other papers"), what the papers that wait need beyond review ("… is decided and only needs Finish; … did not build") and which are with their students ("Blue carbon review is with Priya"); then a chip for each paper that waits (its sign, title, what it needs; a long title cut so the chips wrap in the column) that opens its overview.
- At the right (under it below 900 pt): **Rounds in 2026** (`Timeline`, `TimelineCard`): a lane per paper (title at the left, cut), each round a grey dot at its import with its number over it (a number within 9 pt of the next one's is left out, the later kept), a thick line while it is with you (import to close; to today while open), a dotted one while it is with the student (close to the next import, or to the Finished mark, or to today), the open round's ring (amber when it waits on you, grey with a tick when ready to finish), a failed build's red triangle, a finished paper's diamond; the months under the lanes, Today's line; "Rounds since Sep 2025" when the rounds and today are in two years. A click on a lane opens its paper's overview; its help tag says its status.
- A block per student (two a row from 900 pt, `StudentBlock`): initials, the name, "2 papers · 6 rounds · 15 comments", Profile (a link, `LinkStyle`; grey "Profile ›" until §25; the student's page; the block's header too), a tile per paper (`PaperTile`: its key page over up to two earlier rounds' (`KeyPageStack`, `MapRules.keyPage`, the thumbnails' cache of §21), its **small map** (`MiniMap`, `MiniMapView`: a row per round, newest on top, at most seven, each section where it is in that round shaded with the paper's own three steps in the small maps' own colours, no bands and no outlines, a long appendix folded as on the paper map (v3: §23), "R4" at the left with an amber ring on the open round with something to check, the round's comments at the right, "–" and a dashed red outline for a failed build, plain pages while its sections are pending; no numbers in the cells), the title, the status ("Round 4 · 3 comments, 5 edits to check": "to check" kept since §25, as "6 comments" alone read as the comments in all), "4 rounds since Sep 8 · 11 comments"; a click opens its overview, the context menu is the sidebar's), and "Most comments in **Methods (5)**" (the engine's `land` totals). Unassigned has a block without Profile.
- The small maps' key at the end.

**Your Move** (`YourMovePage`; FEATURES OV-8; the mockup's `yourMoveView`), at most 860 pt wide: a card per paper that waits, the earliest import first: its key page, the title (a click opens its overview), "Priya Nair · Round 3 · imported Oct 13", its status line, and one plain button straight into Review (`moveAction`): **Continue**, **Finish Round N…** (Review, then the Finish sheet once the paper's latest state is in, `pendingFinish`), **Show Log…** (Review shows the failed build); then "With students: Blue carbon review (Priya Nair, since Oct 3)." "Nothing waits on you." when none does.

**A student's page** (`StudentPage`; OVERVIEW-v2 §3.2, C's skeleton with B's Where the time goes; the mockup's `studentView`):
- Initials, the name (24 Bold, `PageType.title`; 28 until §25), "2 papers · 6 rounds since Jun 2 · 15 comments"; at the right (under it below 980 pt) who waits on whom (`HubText.studentLede`: "○ **Reef choruses** is your move: Round 4 came in Oct 16. Shrimp snap detector finished on Jul 20 after 2 rounds.", the ○ amber), the papers by what they need (a failed build, your move, ready, with the student, finished, no rounds), at most four said, else three and "Of 20 other papers, 12 are your move and 8 are with Ana.".
- **Papers**: a card per paper (`PaperCard`, two a row from 860 pt): key page, title (18 Semibold), status, its small map, "4 rounds since Sep 8", "**11 comments**", "7 resolved · 1 kept as is", "3 open" (or "finished Jul 20"); a click opens its overview.
- **Writing profile** (without Topics: they wait for tags, v2; decision 9):
  - **Where the comments land** (`LandBox`, the wider column): "each comment once, where you wrote it"; "Most comments land in Methods (5), then Results (4)."; the engine's `land` as a table, a row per paper and "Both papers" / "All 3 papers", each count with a short bar scaled to the table's largest ("–" for none), the standard sections first (Abstract, Intro., Methods, Results, Disc., Concl.), then the headings that keep their own name while they fit, the rest added up in **Other** (its help tag names them; `LandColumns.fit`); "Finding the sections of n rounds…" while every round is pending. Under it the headings that land in a section of another name ("Preliminaries → Introduction · Limitations → Discussion"), said as "matched by their words (not confirmed yet)" until the advisor has answered, and **Map Headings…** (`MapHeadingsSheet`): each paper's first-level headings with a menu of the six standard sections and "Its own column", filled in with the engine's `suggested_bucket` until answered; Done keeps the answers per paper (`PaperRef.buckets` in papers.json, `setBuckets`) and the numbers are asked for again with them.
  - **Where the time goes** (`TimeBox`; decision 8): "days per round trip"; "Mei’s revisions took 9, 14 and 7 days on Reef choruses; 39 days on Shrimp snap detector. You returned each round in 2–3 days." (`HubText.time`); under each paper's name a row per round trip, "R1 → R2" with a dark bar for your days and a light one for the student's ("you 2 · Mei 9 days"), the open round as an amber outline ("you 3 days so far"); the help tag adds when the student's files last changed (`received`); the key and what the days include.
  - **How comments closed** (`ClosedBox`): "rounds you answered in" (the mockup's caption, "rounds from written to decided", described another count: integration 2026-10-19); per paper the comments closed after 1, 2 and 3+ rounds you answered in, still open and followed up, with short bars; "Each comment once. Followed up: you asked again at least once; 2 were then left untouched. Comments you left open count against nobody. No averages, no comparison with other students."
- The numbers are the engine's `students` (CONTRACT amendment "Overview"), matched to the page by the student's papers.

**Debug hooks** (§13): `--hub all|move|student:<name>` (open at that page; the script starts without a PDF); items `hub all|move|student <name>` (the sidebar's rows; waits for the data: the hub result: page, status, the count and its words, Your Move's lines, each student's numbers, scroll, selection, and `loadMs` since the integration: from the `overview` request to its data in the model), `hubwait`, `hubscroll <y>`, `backfillwait` (until the second engine process is idle), `sidebar` (now the rows in words with each paper's badge or status, the table's rows, its selection, the folded students), `studentclick <name>` (the student's row selected in the sidebar's table), `studentfold <name>`, `editpaper <path>|title=…|student=…`, `renamestudent <old>|<new>`, `finished <path> on|off`, `buckets <path> <key>=<id>,…`, `sheet editpaper <path>|rename <name>|mapheadings <name>|none`, `movebutton <path>`; `nav` names a page as `hub:…` in the history; `window` and `shot` find the window without a PDF view.

**Checked** (2026-10-19): `HierarchyTests` (synthetic: `Fixtures/overview-students.json`, the engine's reply for eight papers it built for four made-up students and one without a student: a four-round paper with comments and edits to check, a finished two-round paper, the kelp paper's merge and rename, a paper ready to finish, one with the student, a failed third round, an open first draft; dates spread over the year): grouping and names, whose move each status is, Your Move's order and counts, the engine's `students` decoded, every sentence and status line, the small maps (rows, counts, failed and pending rows, bands), the timeline (lanes, marks, the year), the sidebar's rows (folded, Review), Map Headings (suggestions, answers, `buckets` with null), the table's columns fitting, which rounds the second process fills, papers.json's optional fields; in the model: one count for the three places, Back and Forward through pages, papers and Review, Your Move's buttons (Finish waits for the state), Finished, the fold, Map Headings' answers, Remove from List going home, names from the engine regrouping; and the three pages drawn at 1204, 1044 and 724 pt in both appearances. Fixed from them: the week's sentence listed every round (15 on the copies; now by paper, at most five), a long title in a chip pushed the timeline out of the window, round numbers a day apart drawn over each other, the comments' table wider than its box with ten columns (now Other), an empty table while sections were pending, the time table's names squeezed, the facts wrapping.

**Review fixes** (2026-10-19, see §21 **Review fixes**):
- **Where the comments land** counts each comment under the heading it had in the round it was written (the engine's `sections[].written_as`; CONTRACT amendment "Overview review fixes"): a section renamed later ("Discussion" → "Interpretation"), or continued under another heading, no longer moves its past comments; the advisor's Map Headings answer for a section still holds for all of its comments. A paper without rounds has no row in it nor in How comments closed. Open: whether "Results and discussion" belongs to Results or Discussion (the word list puts it under Discussion) is the advisor's call.
- **Where the time goes** has a row for a closed round followed by a failed one (to the failed import) and for the failed round ("R2, build failed", "you 3 days so far"), with you as decision 10 says.
- **The lede** is capped (above) and marks a paper that is your move with the amber ○, as the mockup.
- **A failed build's status line**: the ⚠ red, its words in the label's ink (the mockup's), on Your Move, the tiles and the cards.
- **A paper without rounds**: "No rounds yet" once (its status), no facts line under it.
- **Small maps** fold the oldest rounds into one last row ("R1–2", a dotted line) instead of leaving them out.
- **The writing profile**: How comments closed under Where the comments land, Where the time goes beside them, so the columns are about as long until v2's boxes.
- **Timeline**: a round's number has a halo of the card's colour, so it stays legible on the Today rule. All Students has no search item in its toolbar (the mockup's magnifier): Jump to… in the sidebar and ⌘K are the search, a deliberate deviation.
- **Engine**: a hierarchy request reads each round's paragraphs once per run and file (`overview._DIGESTS`), not again on every request once the papers listed had more than 256 rounds.

**Not yet**: Topics and the writing profile's tag parts (v2; built 2026-10-20, §24), a student's year or programme ("PhD, year 3" in the mockup: no student record yet), the key pages' marks on the tiles (the hierarchy asks without marks), dragging a paper onto a student, and reopening at the last page. A real click on a student's chevron and on its row was not seen: a background test window takes no first click, so the debug hooks set the table's selection and the fold directly.

**Integration** (2026-10-19, the overview's v1a and v1b together, OVERVIEW-v2 §7 "检查"; CONTRACT amendment "Overview integration"). `swift test` 435 and the engine's 281 tests pass.
- **The numbers**: an independent script recomputed every round's counts, the threads, every comment's path (state, response, answer, page), the summaries and the students' numbers from ledger.json and analysis.json, and found every section's heading line in the round's PDF by its printed words: 4,400 checks, every one equal but the words of one old round (below); every section start (322, 142 distinct) within 0.017 page units of its printed heading, every placed comment's place (445) within 0.009. What the app shows (`ovtext`: the facts, every cell's count and drawn number, chips, ticks, "carried", the readout, the table and the VoiceOver sentences), compared with the engine's reply for the same copy: 2,659 checks at 1440 and 1280 pt (light and dark runs), no difference and no count left out. Your Move: 3 papers, the oldest import first, the same 3 in the sidebar and All Students' heading, each line's comments to check equal to the ledger's.
- **Pending rounds**: every round of the copies was `pending` (PARSER 8); the second process filled the 19 closed rounds in under 25 s; the open rounds stayed "Finding sections…" after Review's own `prose` had found them, until the next answer (fixed, above: §21 "Pending rounds"; `NavigationModelTests.testARoundFilledInWhileInReviewIsAskedForAgain`, `HierarchyModelTests.testARoundFilledInMakesTheNextRequestNew`).
- **Navigation**: All Students → Your Move → a student → a paper's overview → following a section, then a comment → a double click into Review at its card (p.15 of Round 3) → Back (the overview as left, the comment followed, the strip at Round 3) → Forward (Review with the card and page kept: not rebuilt) → All Rounds; Back and Forward through the pages; the overview's scroll (139.5 at 960 × 640) and All Students' (300) put back; the map's keys after Back. Review: the sidebar 236, the PDF 802 and the panel 400 pt at 1440 (§1), Focus 1.3 times larger, J and K in 45–50 ms, an answer written to the ledger, the next card selected, the overview's readout ("✓ 1 so far") and Your Move's line updated after it.
- **Times**: the engine's `overview`, 106–182 ms for a paper in a new process, 3–6 ms again (marks included), 229 ms for the seven papers and 30 ms again; in the app, the overview's data 130–260 ms after the paper's state was asked (it waits for the state; up to 460 ms the first time), the hierarchy's 57 ms for seven papers (311 ms while the engine started); the map's scene 2.5–22 ms to build and 4–8 ms to draw, drawn again 44–103 ms after a click and 67–78 ms after an arrow key.
- **Screenshots** read at 1440 × 900 and 1280 × 800, light and dark: All Students, Your Move, a student's page, the overview (nothing followed, a section, a comment; a four-round paper) and Review.
- **Fixed**: the open round's pending row (above); "How comments closed" captioned "rounds you answered in", the count it shows (the mockup's "rounds from written to decided" counts every round, and on one copy the two give 18 · 5 against 20 · 3); edits' first words without LaTeX (CONTRACT "Overview integration").
- **Known**: in a round whose change ids could not be matched again (a phase-1 ledger's Round 2), the round's words are known (+66 −31) but no edit's are, so the edits show "no printed words".


## 23. The overview's visual layer v3: folding, quiet segments, small maps, Settings (2026-10-20)
`design/overview/v3/VISUAL.md` with the critique's fixes (the advisor's requests 3–5 of 2026-10-20); the encodings and interactions of §21–22 stay. Colours, cells, chips, ticks, bands, lens, rail and legend: §21 **The paper map** (updated in place). Synthetic data only in the repo: `Fixtures/overview-sonar.json`, a fictional four-round paper of 27–29 pages with References of 3.2 pages and an appendix A–E of 10–12 pages (the design's example; made by a script, not in DATA.md).

**Folding parts** (`OverviewFold.swift`: `FoldKind`, `MapFold`, `FoldPlan`, `PageMap`; VISUAL §5 option A):
- **The parts** of a round (`MapFold.parts`): the engine's 5th `spans` field `part` (front, body, references, appendix) when every row has it (CONTRACT amendment "App, folding parts"); else worked out: References by its heading (References, Bibliography, Literature cited, Works cited), the appendix as the lettered first-level sections (or one called Appendix…) after References, or, without References, a run of them at the end that starts with A. The appendix is its first run of such sections.
- **Decided once per paper** (the critique's fix; VISUAL had it per row): at the unfolded scale, with the round where the part is longest. The appendix folds when it is longer than 3 pages and its block (2 pages wide, never narrower than its words: the chevron, "Appendix A–E", the largest count) is at least a page narrower; then References the same way past 4 pages (a block of 1.5 pages), at the scale the appendix leaves. Every row that has the part then folds it, so a row whose appendix is a page long still shows a block, and rows never mix a block and sections. The page scale for the rest is the largest at which every row fits (`FoldPlan.solve`); when the long label would cost more than three pages beyond the block's two (a narrow window: 960 × 640 on the example) the block says "› A–E". Folding that gains nothing does not happen. On the example the body gets 35 pt a page at 1440 × 900 (24 unfolded), 31 at 1280 × 800, 17 at 960 × 640 (14).
- **Each row** maps its places through its own `PageMap` (its own part's start and end), then `RowScale`: the block starts where the row's appendix does, so blocks of different rows can sit a little apart, and one band joins a block to the block below. A row without sections takes the nearest round's map.
- **The block**: a cell from the part's start to its end, its count the total of its sections (shaded by the paper's steps, from the unfolded counts, so folding never shades the body again), "›" (1.4 pt secondary) and its label (11 Medium secondary), the open round's chip its comments to check; ticks and path dots inside it in proportion. Its help tag: "Appendix A–E · Round 4 · pp. 17–29", its length "folded", its comments (to check, and in which sections), each section's count, "Click to show its sections". VoiceOver: one element, "Appendix A to E, Round 4: 1 comment, 1 to check. Pages 17 to 29. Folded.", its action shows it, "Open in Review" its second.
- **The axis** (the critique's fix: one home for the toggle): under a folded part its pages and **Show** ("pp. 17–29 · Show"), no page number under the block; a part shown at its real length a bracket under its pages, "⌄ Appendix A–E · pp. 17–29 · **Fold**". The lens draws a faint wedge from the part's pages on the strip to its block.
- **Showing it**: a click on the block (or Show, or VoiceOver's action) shows the part's sections at their real length, the map drawn again at once at the new scale (no animation); a second click within the double-click interval counts as the block's, whatever is under the pointer now, and opens Review at the part's first section in that round. Following something inside a folded part shows it first (`AppModel.overviewClicked`: a section, a page mostly in it, a comment that is in it in any round; from the map, the ruler, the strip, the panel, the keys). The shown parts are the overview's place (`OverviewPlace.shownParts`, as `unfold`): Back and Forward keep them; the paper opens folded again from the sidebar or another paper. **Fold** folds it again.
- **Settings › Overview** "Fold the appendix and long reference lists" (one switch for both, the critique's fix; on by default; `foldAppendix` in the app's defaults, never read or saved by a test run; `--fold on|off` for a run). Off: nothing folds anywhere.

**Quiet segments** (VISUAL §6.1): neighbouring sections with no comments, each under 24 pt at the page scale, are one stretch of track without gaps (`Row.quiet`; their bands join too), named like a cell since 2026-10-21 ("Rel. · Disc.", "Related +1": §21 **Cells**). A click and the keys still go section by section, and VoiceOver still has an element per section. The pointer rings the whole segment; its tag: "Round 4 · pp. 13–14 · no comments", the sections' names with the one under the pointer in bold, an edit in it that waits for you ("Limitations: ○ 1 edit to check"), "A click follows Ethics statement (under the pointer)".

**Names** (the advisor's review of 2026-10-20 replaced "names without stubs", VISUAL §4.1 and the critique): the cells' names, the hovered row's names over it and the section ruler show as much as fits, by one rule (`MapRules.label`, §21 **Cells**), down to a letter, and since 2026-10-21 with one stem: the map's (`MapScene.names`). The ruler keeps the numbers: "1 Intro.", "3 Bench.", else "1"; an appendix section its letter and its name as far as it fits, "A Dat.", else "A"; "Refs". **The hovered row's names** (11 pt) go where they fit before the next section; one in the way of a To check chip goes before the chip, else on a line of its own over the chips (`hoverNames`; the UI audit: the sections under a chip, those most needing a name, went unnamed).

**Quiet segments and the scale** (2026-10-20): which sections are quiet is decided at the page scale (each under 24 pt there), before the floor; the segment is then one unit of `MapScale`, so a quiet stretch of four pages is never drawn narrower than a section of a quarter page.

**Small maps** (`MiniMap`, `MiniMapView`; VISUAL §6.2): 8 pt rows on a 14 pt pitch, 1 pt between cells, radius 2, no outlines and no bands (in rows this small they were stripes; the structure is the paper page's), their own steps a little stronger than the map's (`miniTrack`, `mini1`–`mini3`: there are no numbers in them; the dark track lighter than the card), "R4" and the counts 11 pt, the open round with something to check in Semibold after an amber ring. The same folding setting: a block exactly 2 pages wide (1.5 for References), shaded by its busiest section, decided once per paper; neighbouring empty cells under 6 pt join. **Warm stone means comment counts only**: Where the comments land's bars use `mini2`; Where the time goes and the timeline's "with you" the neutral `chartYou` (#7D857E / #8F988F) and `chartStudent` (#D5D8D3 / #3E423E).

**Settings** (⌘,; `Settings.swift`; VISUAL §7 with the critique's fixes): the system's Settings window, five tabs in its toolbar (General `gearshape`, Overview `map`, Reading `book`, Tags `tag`, Data & Storage `externaldrive`; Appearance `circle.lefthalf.filled` was the sixth from §26 to §34, and its icon style is now in General), each a grouped form 560 pt wide. **General**: "When Paperole opens" Where you left off | All Students (a pop-up, as Finder's "New Finder windows show"; `openAtLaunch` in the app's defaults, no "reopen" in any key: that word is the comments'; the place itself, `lastPlace`, is the last-place feature's, TAGS.md §8). **Overview**: the folding switch with two small rows that show it (Folded / Shown), "Show the paper map as" Map | Table (the legend's button, the same setting), and "The small maps on All Students and a student's page fold the same way." **Reading**: "Mark changed paragraphs to read" (§18). **Tags**: the writing tags (TAGS.md §4; built 2026-10-20, §24 **Settings › Tags**). A test run reads and saves none of them. The debug item `settings <tab>` shows the five tabs in a tab view controller with toolbar tabs (as the Settings scene draws them) in a window behind every other.

**The sidebar's leftovers** (§21 **Opening**): Review shown takes the keys for the PDF view (`PDFController.takeKeys` after `setLevel(.review)`), unless a text field has them, so the sidebar's arrows no longer choose papers after Return or a double click opened Review; never while Review is hidden. A double click on the row of the paper open in Review: its first click shows the overview at once (and unfolds the tree under the pointer), and its second click, wherever it lands, brings Review back as it was (`AppModel.returnToReview`, within the double-click interval), the overview it showed leaving no place in the history.

**Debug hooks**: `ovpart show|fold appendix|references`, `setting fold on|off`, `settings [general|overview|reading|tags]`; `ovtext` gives `parts` (foldable, folded, short, the page width, the blocks' widths, the axis's words), each cell's part and each row's quiet segments; `nav`'s `map` the parts folded and shown and the setting.

**Checked** (2026-10-20): `MapFoldTests` (the parts from the engine or worked out, labels and pages, a page map, the plan on the example at three widths: every row folded, one block width, the body's gain, the short label at 960; a round with a one-page appendix folding too; nothing folded when short, off or shown, the shown part's bracket and Fold; the axis and the lens; long References; following inside a folded part through the model, the place, another paper; quiet segments, their hits and tag; the mockup’s visibility rule over the folded and shown example, 456 states; the block's tag and VoiceOver; small maps folded, by the busiest section, and their quiet runs; the setting's flag and that a test run saves none; the four tabs and the Overview switch; the map's colours: panel → band → track → three steps each ΔE00 ≥ 2.3 in one order of lightness, numbers ≥ 6.5:1 and names ≥ 4.5:1 on every step, dark steps of chroma ≤ 4.5, far from amber and Sage, the small maps' the same on the card, the hairlines quieter than any cell), `MapLayoutTests` (three steps, names, the count's place, the ruler, the lens's ends, the visibility rule with blocks), `HierarchyTests` (the small maps' three steps and no bands; the double click on the open paper; `testReviewTakesTheKeys`), `ReadingSettingTests` (the Reading tab). The copies, caches and screenshots were deleted after.

**Map labels and widths** (2026-10-20, the advisor's review: a cell between "Intro." and "2" was blank, many narrow cells showed only their count): the floor and scale of §21 **Cells** (`MapScale`, `RowScale.make(_:drawn:)`), the names as far as they fit (`MapRules.label`, `labels`, `cuts`, `clash`, `CellSpacing`), the ruler by the same rule at the strip's scale, the page axis through the scale. Since the UI audit of 2026-10-21 (below, **Map names after the audits**) the ruler and the hovered row's names take the map's names. The small maps keep the page scale: they have no names or counts (§23 **Small maps**).
